title: "Strict Rust policy rollout"
description: "Violation inventory and sequenced migration plan for rust-repository-policy rust-strict-v1."
---

## Basis

- `main` SHA at slice start: `868ce53519234f879d26a8bd2dffaa30fae2d729`
  (identical to the Phase 1 audit SHA: focused re-diff is empty, no
  audit deltas to reconcile).
- Policy: `tailrocks/rust-repository-policy` `v0.1.3`, tarball sha256
  `104c0d8b3a827875776358f941aa88f1c5837c1009305076af9380f4e3fcda25`
  (verified before install; re-pinned from v0.1.2 `cf688c1e...` after
  slice 7 split 59, tree otherwise untouched).
- Install: release materialized to `.cache/rust-policy/` (gitignored,
  re-materialized in CI before `alint check`); adapter `.alint.yml`
  extends `profiles/rust-strict-v1.yml` plus the one trust-gated
  `policy-gaps` command rule; `schemas/renovate.schema.json` vendored
  to the repo root per the install contract (v0.1.3 copy
  byte-identical to v0.1.2/v0.1.1/v0.1.0: no re-vendor); helper
  `rust-repository-policy` from the v0.1.3 artifact on `PATH`
  (self-reports `0.1.0`: identify by artifact, macos-arm64 sha256
  `f6e0a30d2921b0bf176aea937b491f94f6a68b19238277f57f75cf09a393be54`).
- Tool pins: alint `0.17.0`, helper at release tag, profile
  `rust-strict-v1` (36 native rules + `policy-gaps` = 37 loaded).
- Branch: `rust-policy-rollout`. This slice commits install + inventory
  + plan only. No compliance fixes yet. Do not merge to `main`.
- Reproduce: `alint validate-config .alint.yml` (37 rules),
  `alint check` (native + gaps), `rust-repository-policy check-gaps
  --root .` (authoritative gap detail; alint renders gap findings with
  generic text). Fresh clones need `mise trust` (or
  `MISE_TRUSTED_CONFIG_PATHS`) before either tool runs: both resolve
  `cargo` through mise shims.

## Violation inventory (748 total, 13 of 37 rules failing)

Native `alint check` exit 1: 748 errors, 24 passing / 13 failing rules.
`policy-gaps` count (242) reconciles exactly with the direct helper run.

### Native rules

| Rule | Findings | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `size-file-400` | 368 files | Over-400-line `**/*.rs`, worst: console 67, runtime 46, capsule 39, jackin 39, usage 32, xtask 30, config 21. Includes `vendor/arrayref/src/lib.rs`: `vendor/` has no size exclusion. |
| `tests-dir-present` | 60 | Pre-move noise: with flat layout, `crates/*/*` matches 60 child dirs as pseudo-packages, none with `tests/`. Re-baseline after the S4 move. |
| `layout-no-flat-crate-manifest` | 33 | 32 members + excluded `jackin-lints`, all at `crates/*/Cargo.toml`. |
| `docs-root-only-allowed` | 15 | BRANCHING, COMMITS, CONTEXT, CONTRIBUTING, DEFECT_LEDGER, DEPRECATED, ENGINEERING, HOST_AND_CONTAINER, MISROUTED-RECOVERY-MIGRATION, PRERELEASE, PROJECT_STRUCTURE, PULL_REQUESTS, RULES, TESTING, TODO. README/CHANGELOG/AGENTS/CLAUDE stay (allowlisted). |
| `size-lib-main-150` | 14 | 13 under `crates/` (incl. lints lib, 5 mains) + `vendor/arrayref/src/lib.rs`. |
| `cargo-workspace-inherit-edition` | 4 | The 4 `fuzz/` manifests only (`crates/{config,env,manifest,protocol}/fuzz/`). Native globs select depth 2, so flat members are invisible until the move. |
| `cargo-workspace-inherit-license` | 4 | Same 4 fuzz manifests. |
| `cargo-workspace-inherit-lints` | 4 | Same 4 fuzz manifests. |
| `toolchain-channel-pinned` | 1 | Channel is `1.97.1`, policy pins `1.98.1`. |
| `toolchain-mise-parity` | 1 | No `[tools] rust` literal: mise resolves rust via `idiomatic_version_file`. |
| `ci-build-runs-on` | 1 | Single workflow <RepoFile path=".github/workflows/ci.yml" /> has no `build` job. |
| `cargo-no-patch` | 1 | `[patch.crates-io] arrayref = { path = "vendor/arrayref" }`: security-motivated vendor (checksum-verified against pre-attack lockfile). Removal waits on a trustworthy upstream resolution. |

Passing (24, no action): all `layout-requires-*` (incl. nextest config,
renovate, alint entrypoint), `layout-crates-dir`, `layout-github-dir`,
`layout-root-files`, `layout-no-backup-files`,
`layout-no-merge-leftovers`, `layout-no-deep-crate-manifest`,
`cargo-workspace-resolver` (`"3"` string), `tests-only-rust`,
`tests-no-test-files-in-src`, `docs-dir-present`,
`toolchain-ci-parity` (no CI toolchain pin: tolerated via
`allow_missing_target`), `ci-no-pull-request-target`,
`renovate-config-schema`, `renovate-schema-url`.

## Migration discipline (every slice below)

Each step maps readers/writers/ownership before moving anything;
defines forward plus rollback; sequences expand, migrate, verify, then
contract; keeps steps idempotent with partial failure observable;
verifies old and new paths at each transition; and never performs
destructive contraction implicitly: stop after the requested stage
passes.

## Sequenced plan

### 1. Manifest centralization

- `version.workspace = true` x28 (decision first: `jackin-dev`
  `0.1.52` vs workspace `0.6.4`: align versions or keep the outlier
  with a documented exemption).
- `rustix.workspace = true` x1 (process-directory).
- `[lints] workspace = true` x3 (capsule, process-directory, usage-ffi).
- Centralize-or-exempt the 10 remaining non-workspace deps from the
  Phase 1 audit (capsule libc; config shell-words; console open;
  diagnostics sysinfo + rustc_version; docker bollard; image sigstore;
  usage-ffi boltffi; lints dylint): move into `[workspace.dependencies]`
  or record the exemption with owner and expiry.
- Forward: edit member manifests only. Rollback: revert the commit.
  Verify: `cargo metadata --no-deps` package set unchanged,
  `cargo check --workspace` green, `GAP-INHERIT-*` at zero.
  Clears: `GAP-INHERIT-001/002/003`.

### 2. Toolchain alignment

- Bump `rust-toolchain.toml` channel `1.97.1` -> `1.98.1`; add an
  explicit `[tools] rust = "1.98.1"` pin to <RepoFile path="mise.toml" /> (parity rule
  keeps the two in sync; the current idiomatic-only resolution is
  invisible to it). Decide `rust-version` in root <RepoFile path="Cargo.toml" />
  (`1.97` today): align MSRV with the channel or document the split.
- Forward: pin files only. Rollback: revert. Verify: full `cargo
  check` + nextest suite on the new channel (this is the one step
  whose blast radius is the whole tree; gate on green CI).
  Clears: `toolchain-channel-pinned`, `toolchain-mise-parity`.

### 3. Grouped S4 moves (`crates/*` -> `crates/<group>/<package>`)

- Per group, map readers/writers/ownership first: workspace `members`
  list, CI path filters, docs links, docker contexts, xtask path
  assumptions, <RepoFile path="renovate.json" /> `ignorePaths` (jackin-lints entry),
  CODEOWNERS-style path rules. One group per commit; forward is `git
  mv` + member-list update; rollback is revert of that commit.
- Expand: register the new path in `members` alongside the old.
  Migrate: move the directory. Verify: `cargo metadata --no-deps`
  package set identical, `cargo check --workspace`, layout rules for
  the moved group green, targeted nextest for the group. Contract:
  drop the old path (explicit step, never implicit).
- Move all 33 manifests incl. excluded `jackin-lints`.
- After the last group, re-run the FULL check: native
  `cargo-workspace-inherit-*` and `tests-*` rules go blind-to-live at
  depth 2 and will surface new findings; `tests-dir-present` x60 noise
  collapses to one finding per real crate lacking `tests/`.
- Clears: `layout-no-flat-crate-manifest` (re-baselines `tests-*`,
  `cargo-workspace-inherit-*`).

### 4. Fuzz-crate placement (decision + move)

- The 4 `fuzz/` manifests sit at depth 2 today (12 inherit findings)
  and cannot move to `crates/<group>/<pkg>/fuzz/` (depth 3 trips
  `layout-no-deep-crate-manifest`). Recommended: top-level `fuzz/<pkg>/`
  (cargo-fuzz default shape, outside all `crates/`-scoped rules).
- Map readers first (CI fuzz jobs, docs, cargo-fuzz invocations);
  same expand/migrate/verify/contract rhythm as step 3.
- Clears: `cargo-workspace-inherit-*` x12.

### 5. Patch and vendor removal (waits on upstream)

- `[patch.crates-io] arrayref` + `vendor/arrayref` (1 patch finding, 2
  size findings) exist for a security reason. Forward only after a
  trustworthy upstream resolution lands: swap `vendor/arrayref` for
  the upstream version, verify lockfile + `cargo deny` + full tests,
  then contract by deleting `vendor/` in an explicit commit.
  Security signoff required. Clears: `cargo-no-patch`, vendor size
  findings.

### 6. Test normalization

- `GAP-TEST-001` x147: move each to `tests/` or the sibling `tests.rs`
  in canonical form (`#[cfg(test)] mod tests;`, private, external,
  single attribute). Sequence by crate, biggest files first; each file
  independently verifiable via nextest for its crate.
- `GAP-TEST-002` x5: drop `#[path]`, inline `tests.rs` child modules.
- `GAP-TEST-003` x33: declare the module or delete the file (deletion
  is a contract step: confirm no reader first).
- Post-move `tests-dir-present` remainder: add a `tests/` dir per
  crate (no `.gitkeep` strays: `tests-only-rust`).
- Clears: `GAP-TEST-001/002/003`, `tests-dir-present`.

### 7. Size budgets (long tail, per crate)

- 368 files over 400 lines, 14 `lib.rs`/`main.rs` over 150, 15 crates
  over 6000 code lines, 7 crates over 60 files. Order: console,
  runtime, capsule, usage, jackin, xtask, config, then the rest.
  Per-file splits are idempotent and independently verifiable
  (`alint check` on the crate + its nextest target). Crate splits
  follow the step-3 move discipline with ownership mapping.
- Clears: `size-file-400`, `size-lib-main-150`,
  `GAP-SIZE-001/002`.

### 8. Docs consolidation

- Move the 15 root markdown files to `docs/` (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md,
  README.md, CHANGELOG.md stay). Map readers first: root README links,
  docs-site references, scripts that read these paths. Forward: move +
  fix links; verify: docs build + lychee; no contraction beyond the
  move itself. Clears: `docs-root-only-allowed`.

### 9. CI conformance + gate

- Add a `build` job on `ubuntu-latest` to every workflow (1 today).
  Decide the toolchain pin spelling: keep mise-action (currently
  tolerated by `toolchain-ci-parity` via allow-missing) or add an
  explicit pin the parity regex sees (dtolnay `toolchain:` or `rustup
  toolchain install`) for defense in depth.
- Wire the gate: materialize the pinned policy release into
  `.cache/rust-policy` (checksum-verified), install the helper, run
  `alint check` as a required job.
- Clears: `ci-build-runs-on`.

### 10. Registries + dependency direction

- Author `policy/owned-packages.json` (workspace package names),
  `policy/package-groups.json` (tool/library/support/ffi/lints per
  package), `policy/ci-recipes.json` (expected CI job IDs). Verify
  each against `cargo metadata` / workflow job sets; expect
  `GAP-CI-002/003`, `GAP-DEPS-001/002/003`, `GAP-INV-002/003` to surface
  on first landing and sequence a direction-remediation slice for
  `GAP-DEPS-001` before declaring green.
- Clears: `GAP-INV-001`, `GAP-DEPS-004`, `GAP-CI-001` (then the
  newly surfaced set).

## Slice 4: grouped S4 moves (2026-10-06)

- Base: `40edca21` (slices 2-3 landed: versions + `rustix`
  centralized, unsafe opt-outs narrowed, workspace lints inherited).
  Clone: `/tmp/jackin-groups`, branch `rust-policy-rollout`, pushed,
  NOT merged to `main`.
- Forward: `git mv` all 33 manifests to `crates/<group>/<package>`
  (only the 7 contract groups) + 4 fuzz harnesses to top-level
  `fuzz/<pkg>/`; rewrite every affected path reference; one commit.
  Rollback: revert the commit (pure move + path rewrite, no behavior
  change). Expand/migrate/verify/contract per group move: new paths
  registered in `members` alongside code fixes, `cargo metadata`
  package set compared before/after, gates re-run, old paths dropped
  by the move itself (no leftover flat dirs: `crates/` holds only
  the 7 group dirs afterwards).
- Assumption (flagged): the S4 responsibility table text was not
  reachable from this slice (no copy in policy v0.1.0, the rollout
  record, or memory); group assignment below follows the contract
  group names and each crate's documented responsibility. Likewise
  `policy/auxiliary-workspaces.json` is a proposed registry schema
  for the auxiliary-workspace contract fields (path, kind,
  toolchain, lock, gates); the step-10 registries slice may fold it
  into the canonical registry set.

### Group map + justification

- `apps` (shipped deployables): `jackin` (host CLI, 4 bins),
  `jackin-capsule` (in-container PID 1 control plane, bin + lib).
- `core` (foundational shared libraries): `jackin-core`,
  `jackin-config`, `jackin-env`, `jackin-manifest`,
  `jackin-protocol`, `jackin-brand` (T0 dependency-free identity
  tokens), `jackin-build-meta` (shared build helpers),
  `jackin-process` (shared subprocess transport),
  `jackin-process-directory` (child workdir descriptor boundary).
- `services` (runtime subsystems): `jackin-runtime`,
  `jackin-host` (host-side L2 integration), `jackin-usage`,
  `jackin-telemetry`, `jackin-diagnostics`, `jackin-instance`,
  `jackin-agent-status`, `jackin-isolation`.
- `adapters` (external-system integrations): `jackin-docker`,
  `jackin-image` (derived-image/docker build context),
  `jackin-usage-ffi` (boltffi facade for the macOS app),
  `jackin-oppicker` (1Password picker planning),
  `jackin-omp-store` (external credential-database snapshots).
- `ui` (user interfaces): `jackin-console` (operator console),
  `jackin-tui` (shared product presentation),
  `jackin-launch` (launch-progress TUI).
- `tools` (dev/repo tooling): `jackin-xtask`, `jackin-dev` (local
  PR-verification tool, same family as `pr-trailers`),
  `jackin-pr-trailers`, `jackin-lints` (excluded dylint workspace).
- `testing` (test support): `jackin-test-support`,
  `jackin-otlp-testbed`.

### Fuzz placement (auxiliary-workspace contract)

- `crates/jackin-{config,env,manifest,protocol}/fuzz/` (silently
  nested `[workspace]`s) moved to top-level `fuzz/<pkg>/`
  (cargo-fuzz canonical shape, outside all `crates/`-scoped
  rules). Each keeps its own `[workspace]` + committed
  <RepoFile path="Cargo.lock" />; path deps repointed to
  `../../crates/core/jackin-*`.
- Registered in `policy/auxiliary-workspaces.json`: path,
  kind `cargo-fuzz`, toolchain `inherit:rust-toolchain.toml`
  (stable; a `decode_frames` release build was verified on
  1.98.1 from the new layout), lock path, gate
  `cargo xtask ci-fuzz --package <pkg>`.
- `ci-fuzz` contract directories repointed to `fuzz/<pkg>/`;
  cargo-fuzz needs a parent manifest, so the gate now runs
  `cargo fuzz run --fuzz-dir <dir>` from the repo root (proven
  by `fuzz list` + a full target build) instead of entering the
  harness dir. No CI fuzz jobs exist (unchanged); `.velnor`
  keeps `**/fuzz/**` plus explicit `fuzz/**`.
- `[patch.crates-io] arrayref` + `vendor/arrayref` untouched
  (waits on upstream per step 5); dylint library path updated to
  `crates/tools/jackin-lints`.

## Slice 5: re-pin to policy v0.1.1 (2026-10-06)

- Base: `6cf5d922` (slice 4 grouped moves). Clone:
  `/tmp/jackin-v011`, branch `rust-policy-rollout`, pushed, NOT
  merged to `main`.
- Forward: verify the v0.1.1 tarball sha256, materialize to
  gitignored `.cache/rust-policy/`, run the v0.1.1 helper from the
  artifact (`PATH` override at `.cache/rust-policy-path/`; the
  shared on-`PATH` helper stays v0.1.0 until the rollout lands).
  `.alint.yml` needs no edit (extends path unchanged, still 37
  rules); vendored `renovate.schema.json` is byte-identical across
  the two releases (no re-vendor). This slice commits the doc
  re-pin + re-baselined counts only. No compliance fixes yet.
  Rollback: revert the commit, re-materialize v0.1.0.
- v0.1.1 delta (diffed artifact-to-artifact on this tree):
  `tests-no-test-files-in-src` exempts canonical `src/**/tests.rs`
  + `src/**/tests/**`; `size-lib-main-150` covers exactly the S7
  150-set (`**/src/lib.rs`, `**/src/main.rs`, `**/src/bin/*.rs`,
  `**/src/bin/*/main.rs`, `**/src/**/tests.rs`,
  `**/tests/main.rs`, `**/tests/cases.rs`); helper exempts
  canonical case files from `GAP-TEST-001` and plain-`mod` case
  splits from `GAP-TEST-002` (`#[path]` splits keep firing with a
  canonical-path correction).

  `/tmp/jackin-s7`, branch `rust-policy-rollout`, pushed per
  split, NOT merged to `main`.

  `GAP-SIZE-001` 15 crates over 6000 lines, `GAP-SIZE-002`
  10 crates over 60 files. Real boundaries only
  (usage/console/runtime/capsule/instance/auth first); no
  facades, no false generated labels. File splits precede
  crate splits (a >400-line file cannot move to a new
  crate as-is); the S6-precedented file-count overshoot
  (`GAP-SIZE-002` 7 -> 10 from split output) repeats
  transiently per crate and clears with its crate split.

  module seams, public paths preserved via parent
  re-exports (explicit imports, no globs in prod code),
  `#[cfg(test)]`-gated re-exports for test-only-shared
  items; `nextest list` before/after mapping,
  `fmt`/`clippy`/`check` green, `Cargo.lock` consistent,
  `GAP-TEST` stays 0/0/0.

## Re-pin to policy v0.1.3 (2026-10-07)

- Base: `2837b27f` (slice 7 split 59). Clone:
  `/tmp/jackin-repin`, branch `rust-policy-rollout`,
  pushed, NOT merged to `main`.
- Forward: verify the v0.1.3 tarball sha256
  (`104c0d8b...cda25`), materialize to gitignored
  `.cache/rust-policy/`, run the v0.1.3 helper from the
  artifact (`PATH` override at `.cache/rust-policy-path/`;
  the shared on-`PATH` helper stays v0.1.0 until the
  rollout lands). `.alint.yml` needs no edit (extends
  path unchanged, still 37 rules); vendored
  `renovate.schema.json` is byte-identical across all
  four releases (no re-vendor). This slice commits the
  doc re-pin + re-baselined counts only. No compliance
  fixes yet. Rollback: revert the commit, re-materialize
  v0.1.2.
- v0.1.3 delta (artifact-to-artifact diff on this tree):
  `modules/`, `profiles/`, `schemas/` byte-identical to
  v0.1.2 (native suite unchanged); helper binary only
  (macos-arm64 `189f61cc...` -> `f6e0a30d...`). Per the
  release notes, `GAP-SIZE-001/002` aggregates now count
  production code only: `tests/` dirs, `src/**/tests.rs`
  suites, and `src/**/tests/**` splits are excluded from
  both the code-line and file-count budgets.

### Verify (same tree, v0.1.2 -> v0.1.3)

- Env: mise-shimmed cargo/rustc (`MISE_TRUSTED_CONFIG_PATHS`,
  no global trust write), alint 0.17.0, helper from the
  v0.1.3 artifact (self-reports 0.1.0, macos-arm64 sha256
  `f6e0a30d...93be54`).
- Install gate: `alint validate-config .alint.yml` green
  (37 rules); `policy-gaps` 12 == direct `check-gaps` 12
  (reconciles).
- `check-gaps`: 22 -> 12 (`GAP-SIZE-001` 13 -> 7,
  `GAP-SIZE-002` 9 -> 5; every other gap type 0 both
  sides). Six crates clear all SIZE findings: image
  (6798 lines), core (6742/89), protocol (6337),
  diagnostics (11405/70), isolation (10594), launch
  (8466/68). Config (23317 -> 9979) and telemetry
  (12660 -> 10242) keep `SIZE-001` but clear `SIZE-002`.
- Remaining 12 (production-only): capsule 27499 lines /
  204 files (was 51892/358), console 46910/404 (was
  82949/631), xtask 26273/64 (was 36285/155), runtime
  21499/108 (was 47456/231), jackin 13288/63 (was
  36212/196), config 9979 lines, telemetry 10242 lines.
- Runtime hub exclusion proven by census: 231 total
  `.rs` files minus 123 test-scope files (`tests/`,
  `tests.rs`) = 108, exactly the v0.1.3 `SIZE-002`
  count.
- `alint check`: 182 findings, 26 passing / 11 failing.
  Per-rule: `size-file-400` 136, `docs-root-only-allowed`
  15, `size-lib-main-150` 12, `policy-gaps` 12, inherit
  x3 (lints), `cargo-no-patch`, `ci-build-runs-on`,
  `toolchain-*` x2.
- Raw outputs: `/tmp/jackin-repin-v012-gaps.txt`,
  `/tmp/jackin-repin-v013-gaps.txt`,
  `/tmp/jackin-repin-v013-alint.txt`.

  surface moves to a

  the hub renumbers.
